# #1073 — recovered November OmniRoute dispositions, part 1

## Scope

This source reconciliation records the assigned portion of the existing 1–19 November UTC merged inventory for [#1073](https://github.com/bloodf/durindoor/issues/1073). It is not a selection slate and assigns no product work.

Input only: `/var/tmp/durindoor-omni-nov-metadata.sp127gxv/2026-11-01-19.json`, zero-based slice `[0:177]` after numeric PR-number ordering. The slice contains 177 PRs, #13125 through #18481. The full input contains 886 records. Independently checked the slice boundaries, strictly increasing unique PR numbers, unique head SHAs, UTC merge dates within 2026-11-01 through 2026-11-19, and that every changed-path list has `totalCount` equal to its node count with no further page.

Each row records the merge date, full head SHA, full merge SHA (including repeated values if any), disposition, concise evidence/criteria, and the complete changed-path list. No bulk requests were repeated. Rows that mix verified and unverified runtime behavior remain unresolved. Provider identity and sign-in work is routed to Provider Sign-in. Sensitive paths receive only a high-level static defensive disposition.

The behavior is no longer wholly unverified: the balance-related 402 fallback is already present in this fork, and merged [DurinDoor #2120](https://github.com/bloodf/durindoor/pull/2120) added offline coverage for it (`tests/unit/omni-jina-existing-fetch-fallback.test.js`); see [1073-jina-existing-fetch-fallback.md](1073-jina-existing-fetch-fallback.md). The default web-fetch route and named combos continue past a Jina HTTP 402 to the next provider, and an explicit provider still returns its own status.
